baştaki rahmet ile adım adım gözetip olgunlaştıran Rab çerçevesi bu açıklığın korunduğunu düşündürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000784/B001","root_000849/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_0777b1a6fd23c8353f63","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"94:1:2\",\"root_000784\",[\"B001\"]],[\"94:1:4\",\"root_000849\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Daha sonraki boşalma ve doğrulma çağrısı, açılmış göğsü yalnızca sakinleşen bir iç dünya değil, boşaltılıp yeniden işe koşulan bir kap olarak da düşündürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000784/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_13e76aa62f87a4354b76","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"94:1:2\",\"root_000784\",[\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"İç genişleme, başıboş bir istek alanı değildir; son ayet, bu kapasitenin dünyaya dağılma ihtimalini Rabbine doğru yönlendirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000849/B001","root_001643/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_343bbdb8d3616168a0b6","text":"2. **A load-bearing cavity materially expanded.** Fixed `ص د ر/B001` names the anatomical chest, while fixed `ش ر ح/B002` contributes the material image of flesh spread, sliced, or extended. Immediately afterward, 94:2 names a `و ز ر/B002` heavy load and 94:3 makes the back creak under it through `ن ق ض/B005`; the paired ease statements in 94:5-6 supply relief. The material شرح branch gives expansion, and the chest is the front member of a body whose rear member is overstrained. **Abductive move:** I infer that expanding the front redistributes or increases the body's capacity before unloading the rear; no biomechanics are explicit. **Reading change:** the ayah reads as a bodily reconfiguration for bearing and releasing pressure, not only an affective consolation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000516/B003","root_000575/B001","root_000784/B001","root_000784/B005","root_000784/B006","root_000849/B002","root_001657/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_641293e790421c34c20e","text":"1. The chest opening is a bodily decompression caused by the following load-removal sequence. Lexical evidence: `ش ر ح B001` opening and clarifying, `ص د ر B001` chest as bodily front; later `و ض ع B001` setting down, `و ز ر B002` heavy burden, `ن ق ض B005` creaking under strain, `ظ ه ر B002` back opposite belly. Structural cue: 94:1 names the front/interior; 94:2-3 name the burden and the back strained by it. Assigned role: `شرح` releases the front while `وضع` removes what had crushed the back. Abductive move supplied by me: front expansion and back relief belong to one bodily mechanism of pressure removed from the torso. Reading change: the ayah becomes somatic relief, not only inner consolation. 2. The opening is also interpretive clarification of a hard affair. Lexical evidence: `ش ر ح B001` opening a meaning so it becomes clear; `ص د ر B002` foremost/beginning of an affair or speech, and `ص د ر B004` source from which forms issue; later `ذ ك ر B003/B004/B009` recall, mention on the tongue, and reminder. Structural cue: after the chest is opened, the “mention” is raised in 94:4 and then the repeated maxim of hardship/ease appears in 94:5-6. Assigned role: `صدر` is the internal source or head of speech/meaning, and `شرح` makes what issues from it intelligible. Abductive move supplied by me: the passage treats relief as a cognitive-linguistic opening that allows the burdened condition to be named and remembered differently. Reading change: the ayah reads as the opening of understanding and speech, not only expansion of emotion. 3. The opening has a guarded-cultivation reading. Lexical evidence: `ش ر ح B006` guarding and preserving crops or young palms; `ر ح م B001/B003` tenderness and womb; later `ر ب ب B002` nurture stage by stage. Structural cue: the basmalah’s mercy precedes the chest, while final `رَبِّكَ` names the caretaker toward whom desire turns. Assigned role: the opened chest is vulnerable new growth needing protection. Abductive move supplied by me: opening without guarding would expose the interior; the mercy/lordship frame supplies protection for the opened interior. Reading change: `نشرح لك صدرك` becomes not just making space but preserving what has been opened. 4. A secondary desire reading is activated but remains ambivalent. Lexical evidence: `ش ر ح B005` expansive inclination toward worldly things; later `ر غ ب B001` desire turning toward or away in 94:8. Structural cue: the first ayah opens inward space, while the last ayah directs رغبة specifically `إلى ربك`. Assigned role: `شرح` creates capacity for desire, and 94:8 governs its direction. Abductive move supplied by me: expanded inward capacity can become undirected appetite unless the final ayah orients it. Reading change: the chest-opening includes a latent risk and remedy: expansion is good when desire is turned toward the Lord.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000516/B004","root_000784/B001","root_000849/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_64bc3c9e3357d2e8a61b","text":"1. **Opening an inner source so meaning can issue outward.** Fixed `ش ر ح/B001` is the opening and clarification of a hidden meaning; fixed `ص د ر/B004` is a source from which forms or acts issue. The public `ذ ك ر/B004` mention raised in 94:4 and the twice-articulated relation in 94:5-6 give the structural path interior clarification -> outward formulation. The شرح branch reveals content, while the صدر branch locates its point of issue. **Abductive move:** I infer that inner clarification enables later utterance and public mention; the packet supplies the sequence, not that causal arrow. **Reading change:** opening the chest becomes an epistemic and expressive operation, making an inner source capable of issuing intelligible speech or action.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000784/B001","root_000849/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_a21201e73522a7d84882","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"94:1:2\",\"root_000784\",[\"B001\"]],[\"94:1:4\",\"root_000849\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Açılma, gizli bir anlamı açıklığa çıkarıp söz ve eylemin doğduğu iç kaynağı dışarıya anlaşılır biçimde işler hâle getirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000784/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_b004834a48a9f8500198","text":"94:7 changed the feel of 94:1 most sharply. The opened `صدر` did not remain a quiet interior; `فَرَغْتَ فَٱنصَبْ` made the opening look like emptying a vessel and then setting it upright for use. 94:8 also disciplined the otherwise risky `ش ر ح B005` branch: expansive inclination is not allowed to drift, because رغبة is directed `إِلَىٰ رَبِّكَ`. The later repeated hardship/ease lines made the opening read less like simple comfort and more like a capacity produced inside pressure.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000784/B002","root_000849/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_e9235a1be6d5710fbab2","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"94:1:2\",\"root_000784\",[\"B002\"]],[\"94:1:4\",\"root_000849\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Göğsün açılması, sonraki yük ve gıcırdayan bel anlatımıyla, gövdenin önünü genişletip arkadaki basıncı azaltan maddi bir yeniden düzenleme olarak belirginleşir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000784/B001","root_000784/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_fc929466ac7d40a9c71d","text":"94:2-3 corrected an initially abstract reading of “opening” by forcing the chest into a front/back load-bearing body: the burden and creak made `ش ر ح/B002` materially consequential.
